HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"blocked","docket_ready":false}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_0c87756fe6b561c914fa","text":"89:21 surprised the peg image most: the “fixed” apparatus loses force when the ground that receives pegs is itself crushed.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001620/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_3bc7af2eb1614c803c58","text":"2. **Apparent planted firmness exposed as interceptable.** **Evidence/cue:** `و ت د B003` promises upright planted stability, but 89:13 supplies downward pouring (`ص ب ب B001`) and 89:14 a prepared watching post (`ر ص د B001/B003`). **Mechanism:** fixing resists ordinary displacement, while the later vertical pour and watched path bypass that defense. The packet supplies firmness and counter-movements; I infer their adversarial direction. **Reading change:** the stakes become an ironic claim to permanence whose very fixedness leaves the regime locatable and unable to evade interception.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001620/B001","root_001620/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_52895bd4e3a18c3c88a0","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"89:10:3\",\"root_001620\",[\"B001\",\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Kazıklar, iktidarı toprağa bağlayan ve hâkimiyeti geniş bir alanda sabitleyen bir yönetim düzeneğini simgeler.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001620/B001","root_001620/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_5f03f09bf117098b1694","text":"1. **Pegs as apparatus of fixed domination.** Lexical evidence: `و ت د B001` driven peg; `و ت د B003` planted firmness like a peg; `ر ب ب B001` lordship/authority in the surrounding divine counter-frame. Structural cue: Pharaoh is defined by possession of pegs after Iram’s pillars and Thamud’s carved rock. Assigned role: pegs are the technology of fixing, pinning, and making authority appear planted. Abductive move: I infer that “possessor of pegs” belongs to the same architecture-of-power chain as pillars and rock-cutting. Reading change: the ayah reads as a compact emblem of immobilizing control.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_c4d07e8db0e1a0f8da7d","text":"89:11-12 made the pegs social by linking planted firmness to rule across lands and scaled corruption. 89:13-14 then exposed the surprise in that firmness: a fixed apparatus is easier to locate from a watchpost and cannot answer force that descends rather than pushes laterally.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001620/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_e24afdd5ff79a2324813","text":"2. **Fixity under the sign of coming unfixing.** Lexical evidence: `و ت د B001` fixing pegs; later `د ك ك B001` crushing/demolishing; `ء ر ض B006` sticking to ground and lingering. Structural cue: planted firmness comes before earth itself is pounded level. Assigned role: the peg image prepares a reversal in which the ground no longer secures oppressive structures. Abductive move: I infer that the later earth-event invalidates every earlier technology of anchoring. Reading change: the ayah’s fixedness becomes temporary and ironic.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001620/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_ebd1b4ce44e77be6f7e3","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"89:10:3\",\"root_001620\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Toprağa dikilmiş sağlamlık, yeri değiştirilemez bir güvence olmaktan çıkar; sabit oluşu düzeni görünür ve karşılığa açık hâle getirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001620/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_f634bb543a29d58df32c","text":"1. **Stakes as an apparatus of territorial fixation.** **Evidence/cue:** `و ت د B001/B003` supplies driven pegs and planted firmness. Pillars, rock, valley, and repeated *bilad* surround the epithet, followed by overstepping and multiplied corruption. **Mechanism:** stakes fasten authority to land, serving as the smallest repeatable unit of a wider infrastructure of control.
